Method: 将在线阈值调优建模为序列决策问题：RL智能体摄入流式速率和信号敏感特征摘要，更新触发阈值以在目标本底率容忍带内最大化信号效率。采用分组过滤策略优化（GFPO）适配流式控制，提出两个变体GFPO-F和GFPO-FR，在训练中强制执行本底率可行性约束。

Result: 在模拟LHC运行基准上，对总横向能量（H_T）触发器和异常检测（AD）触发器，容忍时间比例分别提升48%和28%，容忍区间内信号效率累计增益达2%。直接迁移至CMS Run 283408真实碰撞数据（无需微调），H_T和AD的容忍时间比例分别提升56%和28%，且两种触发器均有额外信号效率增益。

Conclusion: 这是首次在真实LHC碰撞数据上实现基于强化学习的触发控制，证明了RL方法在LHC高能物理实时触发场景中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: High-throughput scientific facilities such as the Large Hadron Collider depend on real-time event filtering (\textit{triggering}) under tight constraints on bandwidth, latency, and storage. In practice, trigger menus are largely static and hand-tuned and can become suboptimal as detector conditions, pileup, and background composition drift over time. We cast online threshold tuning as a sequential decision-making problem: a reinforcement learning agent ingests streaming summaries of recent rates and signal-sensitive features and updates trigger thresholds to maximize signal efficiency while tracking a target background rate within a tolerance band. We adapt Group-Filtered Policy Optimization (GFPO) to streaming control and introduce two variants (GFPO-F, GFPO-FR) that enforce background rate feasibility during training. On a benchmark that emulates realistic collider operation, we study two representative triggers: a total transverse energy ($H_{T}$) trigger sensitive to pileup variation, and an anomaly-detection (AD) trigger based on reconstruction loss for rare or non-standard signatures. On Monte Carlo streams, our agent increases the fraction of in-tolerance time intervals by 48\% ($H_T$) and 28\% (AD), with a cumulative gain of up to 2\% in signal efficiency on those in-tolerance intervals. Transferring from simulation to \emph{real} collision data (CMS Run 283408), the same agent, without fine-tuning, achieves a 56\% ($H_T$) and 28\% (AD) in-tolerance improvement over baselines, with further signal-efficiency gain on both triggers. To our knowledge, this is the \emph{first} demonstration of RL-based trigger control on real Large Hadron Collider collision data. Code is available at https://github.com/Zixind/GFPO\_LHC.

</details>


### [117] [EMAgnet: Parameter-Space EMA Regularization for Policy Gradient Self-Play in Large Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23995)
*Tristan Maidment,JB Lanier,Chase McDonald,Nathan Tsang,Eugene Vinitsky,Roy Fox,Albert Wang,Wesley N. Kerr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMAgnet，一种使用指数移动平均策略参数作为正则化目标的自对弈强化学习方法，在两人零和博弈中相比PPO+均匀正则化策略降低了大部分环境下的可利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀分布正则化对所有动作一视同仁，无法区分可行与不可行动作；需要一种能随策略演化自适应调整的正则化目标。

Method: 提出EMAgnet，使用上一轮策略参数的指数移动平均（EMA）作为正则化目标，替代均匀分布正则化，提供随代理策略改进而演化的自适应正则化目标。

Result: 在大部分两人零和基准环境中，EMAgnet相比PPO自对弈+均匀正则化（线性/幂律退火调度）取得了更低的可利用率；在包含严格占优策略的游戏中表现一致更好。

Conclusion: EMAgnet的自适应正则化策略比均匀分布正则化更有效，尤其适合存在探索挑战和大量严格占优策略的场景。

Abstract: Recent work has established that regularized policy gradient methods such as PPO, when used in self-play, can match or exceed specialized game-theoretic algorithms for solving two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. The uniform distribution has emerged as a strong policy regularization target for this purpose, but it regularizes equally toward all actions regardless of their viability. We introduce EMAgnet, which instead regularizes toward an exponential moving average (EMA) of the last-iterate policy's parameters, providing an adaptive regularization target that evolves with the agent's improving strategy. We evaluate EMAgnet on both standard two-player zero-sum benchmarks and modified benchmarks with exploration challenges and large numbers of strictly dominated strategies. Relative to PPO self-play with uniform-magnet regularization under both linear and power-law annealing schedules, EMAgnet achieves lower exploitability in the majority of tested environments, with consistent performance gains across games containing strictly dominated strategies.

</details>


### [118] [Cyclic Denoising Reveals Ultrastable Memories in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24000)
*Rishabh Sharma,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出循环去噪（cyclic denoising）作为一种针对图像扩散模型的提取攻击方法，通过在受控噪声幅度下重复进行前向和反向扩散，揭示训练数据中的记忆样本（如库存照片、品牌水印等）。该方法无需梯度、权重检查或提示词，在Stable Diffusion v1.4和像素空间DDPM上均有效，并观察到类似屈服转变的动力学现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的记忆化审计方法（如生成-过滤攻击）依赖大规模条件生成和后处理过滤，效率有限且需要先验知识。受无序固体中随机组织现象的启发，作者希望设计一种无需条件、无需梯度、无需先验知识的物理启发式方法，系统性地探测生成模型中的记忆化区域。

Method: 提出循环去噪协议：对图像反复施加受控噪声幅度的前向扩散（加噪）和反向扩散（去噪）循环。通过调节噪声幅度控制动力学行为，低幅度导致平凡不动点，高幅度引发重构和吸引子跳跃。该方法仅需采样器级控制，无需梯度、权重访问、提示词或训练数据先验知识。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4和像素空间DDPM上验证了该现象。发现：1) 深度吸引子对应记忆化的训练图像（库存照片、品牌水印、网络爬虫伪影）；2) 这些吸引子在近完全破坏后仍能再生，且经数千次循环后持续存在；3) 观察到类似屈服转变的相变：低幅度循环产生平凡吸收不动点，高幅度引发盆地跳跃和结构化记忆化吸引子中的长时间捕获；4) 发现层次性部分吸收、提示稳定化盆地以及跨初始条件的吸引子集普适性。

Conclusion: 循环去噪不仅是探测生成模型景观的物理启发式探针，也是实用的记忆化审计工具，对隐私保护、版权合规和模型指纹识别具有重要影响。该方法揭示扩散模型可能以超稳定吸引子形式记忆训练数据，且这些记忆在极端破坏下仍能恢复。

Abstract: We introduce cyclic denoising -- repeated forward and reverse diffusion at controlled noise amplitudes -- as an extraction attack for image diffusion models. Inspired by random organization in disordered solids, cyclic denoising exposes regions of the learned distribution that are largely inaccessible to standard sampling. The dynamics drive samples toward attractors with a broad stability spectrum. The deepest attractors are ultrastable: they regenerate after near-total corruption and persist through thousands of noising-denoising cycles. Many of these attractors correspond to memorized training images, including stock photographs, brand watermarks, and web-crawl artifacts. The attack requires only sampler-level control, with no gradients, weight inspection, prompts, captions, or prior knowledge of the training data. Unlike generate-and-filter attacks, which rely on large-scale prompted generation and post-hoc similarity or membership-inference filtering, our main protocol is fully unconditioned. We demonstrate the phenomenon in Stable Diffusion v1.4 and in a pixel-space DDPM, showing consistent behavior across latent- and pixel-space diffusion models. Across noise amplitudes, we observe a yielding-like transition: low-amplitude cycling produces trivial absorbing fixed points or limit cycles, while larger amplitudes induce rearrangements, basin hopping, and long-lived trapping in structured memorized attractor basins. We also observe hierarchical partial absorption, prompt-stabilized basins, and cross-initial-condition universality of the recovered attractor set. Our results therefore show that cyclic denoising is both a physics-inspired probe of generative landscapes and a practical tool for memorization auditing, with implications for privacy, copyright compliance, and model fingerprinting.

</details>


### [119] [Fast and Slow Variational Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24007)
*Subarnaduti Paul,Yohan Jung,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Siddharth Swaroop,Thomas Möllenhoff,Martin Mundt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVON优化器，通过将VCL框架中的快速和慢速适应机制结合到IVON优化器中，实现了持续学习中的稳定性与可塑性平衡，在多个持续学习任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度网络缺乏持续的适应机制，而神经科学中的快速和慢速适应机制虽然能平衡稳定性和可塑性，但尚未在常用优化器中得到有效整合。

Method: 基于VCL框架，将过去的后验合并作为未来的先验，实现慢速适应以减缓知识漂移；合并后的后验作为VCL更新的先验实现快速权重更新，并集成到IVON优化器中形成CoVON优化器。

Result: CoVON不仅在现有的VCL优化器上有一致的改进，而且在领域增量学习、持续预训练和大语言模型微调等任务上优于其他权重正则化策略。

Conclusion: 通过将快速和慢速适应机制无缝集成到与Adam形式和成本几乎相同的IVON优化器中，CoVON为持续学习提供了一种简单而有效的优化方案。

Abstract: Continual learning remains a major challenge for modern deep networks, partly because commonly used optimizers lack inherent mechanisms for continual adaptation. One such natural mechanism is fast and slow adaptation to balance stability and plasticity. This mechanism has deep roots in neuroscience and biology, but there is no consensus on how to best incorporate it in commonly used optimizers. Here, we show that this can be easily done via the VCL framework, where past posteriors are used as priors in the future. Our key idea is to incorporate slow adaptation via merging of past posteriors to slow down the drift in the knowledge as learning progresses. The merged posterior is then used as the prior in the VCL update to implement the fast-weight updates. These steps can be seamlessly implemented in the IVON optimizer, whose form and costs are nearly identical to that of Adam. We call this new optimizer the Continual IVON (CoVON) optimizer and show that it not only consistently improves over existing VCL optimizers, but also performs better than other weight-regularization strategies across domain-incremental learning, continual pre-training, and fine-tuning of large language models.

</details>


### [120] [You Don't Need to Run Every Eval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24020)
*Yuchen Zeng,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现前沿模型的133个基准测试得分矩阵近似秩为2，即模型性能主要由两个因素决定。基于此开发了BenchPress方法，用少量基准（如5个）即可高精度预测模型在全部基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估需要运行大量基准测试，成本高昂。作者希望探究是否所有评估都是必要的，能否通过少量基准来预测模型在全部基准上的表现，从而节省评估成本。

Method: 收集84个前沿模型在133个基准上的得分矩阵（2,604个单元格，填充率23.3%），发现矩阵近似秩为2。设计BenchPress：一种logit空间的秩-2矩阵补全方法，可恢复隐藏得分，并带有置信度层来判断预测的可信度。

Result: BenchPress恢复隐藏得分误差在4.6分以内；5个基准子集{GPQA-D, HLE, Codeforces, MMLU-Pro, ARC-AGI-1}可恢复完整分数卡，误差3.93分；更经济的4个基准子集预测误差4.55分。

Conclusion: 模型评估存在高度冗余，少量精心挑选的基准即可高效预测模型在大量基准上的表现，显著降低评估成本。

Abstract: A modern model release reports scores on 40+ benchmarks and the same evaluations were run many more times before it: to track training progress, compare design choices, and select the checkpoint for the release. But do we need to run every eval? We compile a public score matrix of 84 frontier models on 133 benchmarks (2,604 cells, 23.3% filled) and find it is approximately rank-2: a model's scores across all 133 benchmarks are largely determined by just two numbers. We confirm this in two ways: scores hidden from the matrix are best recovered using two factors, and two factors already explain over 90% of the variation among models on the benchmarks they share. Building on this, we design BenchPress: a logit-space rank-2 matrix completion method that recovers held-out scores to within 4.6 points, and a confidence layer that says when each prediction can be trusted. Using BenchPress, we find a subset of five benchmarks {GPQA-D, HLE, Codeforces, MMLU-Pro, ARC-AGI-1} that can recover the rest of a model's public scorecard to within 3.93 points. For a tighter inference budget, a cheaper set {GPQA-D, MMLU-Pro, Aider Polyglot, MATH-500, AIME 2026} can predict a model's evals to within 4.55. We release the score matrix, the BenchPress code, and an interactive tool that predicts any model's score on any benchmark.

</details>


### [121] [Information-Theoretic Classifier-Free Guidance with Adaptive Schedule Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24025)
*Haobo Chen,Xiangxiang Xu,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信息论的CFG调度优化框架，通过清洁端点参考指定一致性-覆盖率的权衡，并优化引导采样器产生的实际分布，在ImageNet-512和COCO上实现了优于恒定引导的权衡效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CFG通过增加引导权重提升条件一致性，但强引导会降低多样性和分布覆盖率，且CFG诱导的分布并非简单的时间固定倾斜分布，如何在整个逆向轨迹中控制一致性-覆盖率权衡尚不明确。

Method: 提出信息论框架，使用清洁端点参考指定期望的一致性-覆盖率权衡，优化引导采样器产生的实际分布；推导轨迹级公式，通过样本和分数评估估计目标，避免显式密度估计。

Result: 在ImageNet-512（EDM-XXL）和COCO（SD-XL）上，学习到的调度在权衡上达到竞争性或更优的结果，并且能够根据噪声级别选择性分配引导强度。

Conclusion: 所提出的CFG调度优化方法有效解决了引导强度与多样性之间的权衡问题，通过动态调度引导权重，在不同噪声级别上实现更优的条件一致性和分布覆盖。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved strong performance in image, text-to-image, and video generation, where conditional generation is often controlled by classifier-free guidance (CFG). CFG improves condition consistency by increasing a guidance weight, but stronger guidance typically reduces diversity and distributional coverage. It remains unclear how this consistency-coverage trade-off should be controlled across the reverse trajectory, since the distribution induced by CFG is not simply the fixed-time tilted distribution given by the guided score field. To address this issue, we propose an information-theoretic framework for CFG schedule optimization. Our approach uses a clean endpoint reference to specify the desired consistency-coverage trade-off, while optimizing the actual distribution induced by the guided sampler toward this reference. We derive trajectory-level formulas to estimate the objective from samples and score evaluations, avoiding explicit density estimation. On ImageNet-512 with EDM-XXL and COCO with SD-XL, the learned schedules achieve competitive or improved trade-offs over constant guidance and allocate guidance selectively across noise levels.

</details>


### [122] [RoPE-Aware Bit Allocation for KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24033)
*Fengfeng Liang,Yuechen Zhang,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Block-GTQ是一种基于RoPE感知的KV缓存量化比特分配方法，通过分块能量评分和贪心比特分配，在低比特量化下显著提升长上下文检索、理解和推理性能，并实现高效打包服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特KV缓存量化器将每个缓存键视为平坦向量，忽略了RoPE下键对注意力logit的贡献可分解为位置相关的二维频率块和，导致量化误差分配不均。需要一种RoPE感知的逐块比特分配方法。

Method: 提出Block-GTQ，基于TurboQuant-MSE构建。对每层每KV头，为每个RoPE块计算无标签能量分数，通过边际增益贪心分配整数比特宽度，实现分块级比特分配。

Result: 在10模型诊断面板上，每层MAE降低32-80%；K2V2下Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct的NIAH六任务平均从70.6提升至97.4，LongBench-EN从36.87提升至53.31；DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上K3V2的AIME 2024/2025得分51.7/37.5（fp16为54.2/37.9），均匀TQ-MSE崩溃至0.0/0.0。K3V3实现3.24x压缩，1.34x加速，128K下峰值内存从56.31GB降至19.85GB。

Conclusion: Block-GTQ通过RoPE感知的块级比特分配，在同等比特预算下显著优于均匀量化，能有效保持RoPE查询-键logit保真度，并转化为实际的下游任务增益，同时支持高效打包部署。

Abstract: Existing low-bit KV-cache quantizers often treat each cached key as a flat vector. Under RoPE, however, a key's contribution to a future attention logit decomposes into a position-dependent sum over two-dimensional frequency blocks. This makes key-cache quantization a block-wise bit-allocation problem: high-energy RoPE blocks are more sensitive to quantization error and should receive more bits. We introduce Block-GTQ, a RoPE-aware bit allocator for key-cache quantization built on TurboQuant-MSE(TQ-MSE). For each layer and KV head, Block-GTQ computes a label-free energy score for each RoPE block and greedily allocates integer bit widths by marginal gain. Under matched K/V bit budgets, Block-GTQ better preserves RoPE query-key logits on a ten-model diagnostic panel, cutting per-layer MAE by 32-80% at 2 and 3 b/dim K-only quantization and winning all 367/367 layer comparisons against uniform TQ-MSE. These fidelity gains translate to stronger downstream long-context retrieval, understanding, and reasoning. At K2V2 on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Block-GTQ raises the six-task NIAH average from 70.6 to 97.4, and the LongBench-EN average from 36.87 to 53.31. On AIME 2024/2025 with DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, without an fp16 recent-key buffer, Block-GTQ at K3V2 scores 51.7/37.5, close to fp16's 54.2/37.9, whereas uniform TQ-MSE collapses to 0.0/0.0. We further implement a packed-cache serving path. On a single H800 GPU with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, packed K3V3 achieves 3.24x KV-cache compression with fp16-comparable quality, runs 1.34x faster than fp16 FlashAttention2 at 128K context, reduces peak memory from 56.31 GB to 19.85 GB, and remains feasible at 256K and 512K where fp16 OOMs. Code is available at https://github.com/JIA-Lab-research/blockgtq.

</details>


### [123] [Rapid FinFET Modelling Using an Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24046)
*Amit Sarkar Suman Sau,Swagata Mandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自编码器（AE）的机器学习框架，用于高效建模FinFET。通过校准BSIM-CMG模型生成I-V特性数据集，训练自编码器将完整I-V曲线压缩到低维潜空间，并显式引入VDS作为输入特征以捕捉偏置依赖变化。该模型能重构完整I-V曲线并直接提取VTH、SS、峰值gm等关键器件指标，实现了数据驱动的紧凑模型在小样本下高精度建模，适用于快速器件表征与电路级仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FinFET建模依赖物理基紧凑模型（如BSIM-CMG），参数提取复杂且耗时长。本文旨在利用数据驱动方法，通过自编码器直接从表征数据中学习器件行为，实现高效、准确的器件建模，以加速器件特性分析和电路仿真流程。

Method: 1) 校准BSIM-CMG模型生成ID-VG特性数据集；2) 训练自编码器将完整I-V曲线压缩到低维潜空间，隐式编码关键器件物理；3) 显式将VDS作为输入特征纳入模型，增强对偏置依赖变化的捕捉能力；4) 通过潜空间表示重构完整I-V曲线，并从中提取VTH、SS、峰值gm等关键指标。

Result: 训练后的自编码器能够准确重构完整的I-V曲线，并成功提取阈值电压（VTH）、亚阈值摆幅（SS）和峰值跨导（gm）等关键器件指标，在仅需少量训练数据的情况下即达到高精度。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的紧凑模型（基于自编码器）能够从实际表征数据中学习，以极少的训练数据实现高精度器件建模，为快速器件表征、建模及电路级仿真提供了有力的工具。

Abstract: This work presents a machine learning framework that leverages an autoencoder (AE) for the efficient modeling of FinFET. We first calibrated a BSIM-CMG model to generate a dataset of current-voltage (ID-VG) characteristics. This data was used to train an autoencoder that compresses full I-V curves into a low-dimensional latent space, which intrinsically encodes key device physics. A key innovation is the explicit incorporation of parameter such as drain to source voltage (VDS) as an input feature, enhancing the model ability to capture bias dependent variation. The trained model successfully reconstructs full I-V curves and directly extracts critical device metrics including threshold voltage (VTH), subthreshold slope (SS), and peak transconductance (gm). This approach demonstrates that data driven compact models, built from actual characterization data, can achieve high accuracy with minimal training data, providing a powerful tool for rapid device characterization, modelling and circuit level simulation.

</details>


### [124] [RAVEN: A Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network for Financial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24062)
*Cheng He,Zhenyu Guan,Xijie Liang,Defu Lian,Jiajia Li,Enhong Chen,Patrick P. C. Lee,Geng Hu,Zehao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAVEN模型，通过变上下文窗口的专家混合框架（MoE）解决金融时间序列中固定窗口与最优回溯长度不匹配的问题，采用CIT机制动态确定上下文长度、全局压缩表示(GCR)保持全局一致性、相关性感知加权(CAW)处理专家输出的结构化重叠，在股指和基金销售预测上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SOTA时间序列模型在金融场景中存在关键局限性：固定上下文窗口与非平稳价格过程中时变的最优回溯长度不匹配，导致模型无法自适应地选择合适的历史信息。

Method: 构建嵌套连续窗口层次结构，通过CIT机制按逆时间顺序累积重要性得分确定窗口边界；不同窗口路由至不同尺度专家；GCR分支并行处理完整上下文保持全局时间一致性；CAW机制对齐变长专家输出并惩罚余弦相似度。

Result: 在HS300和S&P500上Pearson相关系数分别提升9.2%和20.2%；基金销售预测MSE降低18.2%；在PEMS交通数据集16项指标中获得14项最优。

Conclusion: RAVEN通过自适应确定上下文窗口长度，有效解决了金融时间序列中非平稳性和时变依赖性问题，在多个任务上超越现有方法。

Abstract: Financial time series forecasting presents structural challenges absent from standard benchmarks. Log-returns are non-stationary, exhibit exceptionally low signal-to-noise (SNR) ratios, and are governed by regime-dependent temporal dependencies. We identify a key limitation of state-of-the-art (SOTA) time series models in financial settings. A fixed context window is mismatched to the time-varying optimal look-back of non-stationary price processes. We propose the Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network (RAVEN), a Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to adaptively determine the temporal context for each input sample. Instead of relying on a fixed look-back horizon, RAVEN constructs a hierarchy of nested contiguous windows whose lengths are determined by the data itself. Specifically, RAVEN scores patches by learned importance in reverse chronological order and applies the Cumulative Importance Thresholding (CIT) mechanism to derive nested prefix windows, each routed to a scale-specialized expert. A Global Compressed Representation (GCR) branch runs in parallel over the full context, preserving global temporal coherence that local experts cannot guarantee. Because the nested routing induces structured overlap among expert inputs, we introduce a Correlation-Aware Weighting (CAW) to align variable-length expert outputs and penalize pairwise cosine similarity prior to aggregation. Experiments on cumulative log-return prediction (HS300, S&P500) and fund sales forecasting demonstrate that RAVEN achieves SOTA performances, improves Pearson correlation by 9.2% on HS300 and 20.2% on S&P500, and reduces MSE by 18.2% on fund sales forecasting, while achieving the best results in 14 of 16 metrics on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [125] [Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24084)
*Liwen Zheng,Haiyun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于块的策略漂移门控（Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating），通过学生策略自身新旧版本的log概率差异，对同策略蒸馏（OPD）中的位置损失进行重新加权，在不改变教师信号和 rollout 策略的情况下，显著提升推理任务求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略蒸馏（OPD）使用采样令牌方式在长程推理任务上表现脆弱，局部教师支持匹配虽能修复但仍有改进空间；此外，OPD在复用rollout时存在新旧策略漂移问题，需轻量级控制信号。

Method: 计算行为学生与当前学生在采样令牌路径上的log概率漂移，按固定块（如64 token）聚合，进行detach和均值归一化后作为门控权重，重新加权OPD的位置损失。不改变教师目标、教师Top-K支持或rollout策略。

Result: 在Qwen3数学推理基准的6个变体上，使用200步统一训练预算，固定64-token块门控将采样令牌OPD的pass@8均值从0.4978提升至0.5160（AIME24、AIME25、MATH500、AMC23）。在Teacher-TopK/LSM设置下，Block64取得最佳四基准平均pass@8。

Conclusion: 局部新旧策略漂移可作为复用OPD rollout的有效控制信号，块级门控是一种简单且能提升求解率鲁棒性的默认方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student policy using teacher signals computed on trajectories sampled by the student itself. Recent work shows that sampled-token OPD can be fragile on long-horizon reasoning tasks and that local teacher-support matching is a simple and effective repair. This paper introduces blockwise policy-drift gating, a lightweight student-only old-current drift controller for OPD under rollout reuse. The method computes log-probability shifts between the behavior student and the current student on the sampled token path, aggregates these shifts over fixed blocks or spans, and uses the resulting detached, mean-normalized gates to reweight OPD position losses. It does not change teacher targets, teacher top-K supports, or the rollout policy. In a six-variant Qwen3 math reasoning benchmark with a uniform 200-step training budget for all trained variants, we use pass@8 as the primary problem-level solve-rate metric. Fixed 64-token block gating improves sampled-token OPD mean pass@8 from 0.4978 to 0.5160 across AIME24, AIME25, MATH500, and AMC23. On Teacher-TopK/LSM, Block64 gives the best four-benchmark mean pass@8 among trained students. The results identify local old-current policy drift as a practical control signal for reused OPD rollouts and motivate block-level gating as a simple default for improving solve-rate robustness.

</details>


### [126] [NeuroSonic: Conditional Flow Matching for EEG-to-Speech Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24087)
*Wenhao Gao,Yifan Wang,Yijia Ma,Carl Yang,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroSonic提出了一种基于条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）的EEG到语音重建框架，通过学习确定性概率流速度场将噪声污染的声学状态传输到干净语音，避免了不稳定的波形回归和随机多步生成，在CineBrain和EAV基准上显著优于GAN、扩散和均值流基线方法，感知质量提升达26.3%。该方法将EEG与音频嵌入共享令牌空间，通过时间条件门控Transformer参数化传输常微分方程，在伪影密集段表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头皮EEG信号弱、空间弥散且高度可变，而语音具有强谐波和时间结构的相干声学轨迹，两者之间的不匹配导致直接波形回归不稳定，随机多步生成方法对伪影依赖和受试者变异性敏感，现有方法难以实现稳定、高质量的EEG到语音重建。

Method: 提出条件流匹配框架NeuroSonic，学习从噪声污染声学状态到干净语音的确定性概率流速度场；将EEG与音频嵌入共享令牌空间，使用时间条件门控Transformer参数化传输常微分方程来实现轨迹演化。

Result: 在CineBrain和EAV基准上，NeuroSonic在分布真实性、频谱保真度和感知质量方面均优于GAN、扩散和均值流基线，整体感知质量提升最高达26.3%，伪影密集段性能优势最为明显。

Conclusion: 确定性条件传输为EEG驱动的语音重建提供了一种稳定且有效的方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous speech from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains fundamentally challenging. EEG provides a weak, spatially diffuse, and highly variable measurement of distributed cortical activity, whereas speech is organized as a coherent acoustic trajectory with strong harmonic and temporal structure. The resulting mismatch makes waveform regression unstable and causes stochastic multi-step generation to be sensitive to artifact-dependent conditioning and subject variability. We introduce NeuroSonic, a conditional flow-matching framework for EEG-to-speech reconstruction. Instead of predicting waveforms directly or refining them through stochastic denoising, NeuroSonic learns a deterministic probability-flow velocity field that transports a noise-corrupted acoustic state toward clean speech under EEG conditioning. EEG and audio are embedded into a shared token space and processed by a time-conditioned gated Transformer that parameterizes the transport ordinary differential equation. This formulation models trajectory evolution explicitly while avoiding iterative stochastic sampling. We evaluate NeuroSonic on the CineBrain and EAV benchmarks under cross-subject evaluation. Across both datasets, the proposed method improves distributional realism, spectral fidelity, and perceptual quality over representative GAN-, diffusion-, and mean-flow baselines, with up to a 26.3\% gain in overall perceptual quality. The performance gap is most evident in artifact-heavy segments, where conditioning variability is strongest. These findings indicate that deterministic conditional transport provides a stable and effective formulation for EEG-driven speech reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/Y-Research-SBU/NeuroSonic/ .

</details>


### [127] [FedUP: One-Shot Federated Unlearning via Centroid-Guided Plug-in Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24113)
*Feihong Nan,Zhengyi Zhong,Pan Wang,Weidong Bao,Xiongtao Zhang,Quan Wen,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedUP提出一种基于可插拔过滤器的单次联邦遗忘框架，通过冻结原始模型参数并在服务器端使用差分隐私保护的类质心样本训练过滤器，实现秒级遗忘延迟、减少非目标知识损失，并支持遗忘知识的可逆恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法面临非目标知识损失与高请求延迟之间的持续困境，无法同时满足合规要求与模型性能的平衡。

Method: 提出FedUP框架，采用轻量级可插拔过滤器作为"知识漏斗"；冻结原始模型参数，在服务器端使用差分隐私（DP）保护的类质心样本训练过滤器；实现单次通信的联邦遗忘。

Result: 在多种图像和文本任务上，FedUP有效减少了非目标知识损失，实现了优越的遗忘精度和效率，遗忘延迟从分钟级降至秒级。

Conclusion: FedUP通过可插拔过滤器架构解决了联邦遗忘中效率与精度的矛盾，支持可逆性，为合规遗忘提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) is critical for complying with legal mandates like the right to be forgotten in decentralized systems, yet current methods face a persistent dilemma between non-target knowledge loss and high request latency. To resolve these issues, we propose FedUP, a one-shot federated unlearning framework utilizing lightweight pluggable filters that act as a "knowledge funnel" to screen out target data while preserving original model performance. By freezing original model parameters and training filters at the server side using differentially private (DP)-protected class centroid samples, FedUP bypasses the need for multi-round client-server communication and complex retraining, reducing unlearning latency from minutes to mere seconds. Additionally, the framework's pluggable architecture ensures inherent reversibility, enabling the seamless restoration of forgotten knowledge by simply removing the filters. Extensive experiments on diverse image and text tasks demonstrate that FedUP effectively reduces non-target knowledge loss and achieves superior unlearning precision and efficiency across various scenarios. Code is available at: https://github.com/suows/FedUP-code.

</details>


### [128] [When Top-1 Fails: Calibrating LoRA Monitors for Masked Diffusion LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24119)
*Lucky Verma,Pratik Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明离散扩散语言模型(DLM)的PEFT微调中，top-1 argmax浓度作为崩溃预警指标无效（零精度），原因是预平衡饱和；而最大LoRA梯度范数作为参数侧信号，在LLaDA族上对顶层损失配置实现了P=0.68、F1=0.79的预警性能。建议弃用top-1，改用每族校准的最大梯度阈值进行早期检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型(DLM)微调中，去噪时间置信度监测器被直接用于PEFT训练的崩溃预警，但其有效性未经严格测试。本文旨在检验这些监测器（特别是top-1 argmax浓度）在LoRA/PEFT场景下的实际预警能力，并探索更可靠的替代信号。

Method: 在816个LoRA/PEFT配置（来自三个DLM家族）上测试top-1 argmax浓度作为崩溃预警指标；随后评估最大LoRA梯度范数，并在LLaDA族上进行池化留出验证，使用训练优化阈值确定顶层损失配置。

Result: top-1浓度预警在所有配置下均触发，但0/816实际崩溃（200步视界内），精度为零。最大LoRA梯度范数在LLaDA族上对顶层损失配置达到P=0.68、F1=0.79，高于全正top-1基线。自回归对照和跨族阈值失效将该结果限定于短视界DLM-LoRA检测场景。

Conclusion: DLM的PEFT微调中不应使用top-1浓度作为崩溃预警，应改用每DLM族校准的最大梯度阈值进行早期训练监测。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language model (DLM) fine-tuning inherits inexpensive diagnostics from denoising-time confidence monitors, but their PEFT-training meaning is untested. We test top-1 argmax concentration as a collapse warning. Across 816 LoRA/PEFT configurations from three DLM families, the warning fires for every configuration while logs record 0/816 actual collapses at the 200 step horizon, giving zero precision. The cause is pre-equilibrium saturation: top-1 concentration is already high before optimization and quickly becomes insensitive to final training stability. We then evaluate max LoRA gradient norm, a parameter-side signal that samples gradient routing rather than token concentration. On a pooled held-out LLaDA-family split, a train-optimized threshold identifies top-decile final-loss configurations with precision 0.68 and F1=0.79, above the all-positive top-1 baseline even at the lower split-bootstrap confidence bound. Autoregressive controls and cross-family threshold failures bound the result to short-horizon DLM-LoRA inspection rather than a universal collapse detector. Workflow: drop top-1 as a PEFT alarm, log max-gradient early in training, and calibrate thresholds per DLM family before routing runs for inspection.

</details>


### [129] [Holistic Data Scheduler for LLM Pre-training via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24133)
*Chenhao Dang,Jing Ma,Mingjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了全息数据调度器(HDS)，一种基于强化学习(Soft Actor-Critic)的在线数据混合框架，通过多目标奖励函数(数据质量、跨域损失影响、权重范数)动态调整LLM预训练数据配比，在The Pile基准上以44%更少迭代达到最优方法困惑度，MMLU 0-shot任务提升7.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线数据混合方法仅从单一优化视角出发，无法满足LLM预训练从多维度考虑动态数据组成的复杂需求。

Method: 将数据调度问题建模为连续控制空间的强化学习问题，采用Soft Actor-Critic算法探索高维策略空间，设计融合数据驱动、损失驱动和模型驱动三个维度的多目标奖励函数。

Result: 在The Pile上以44%更少训练迭代达到次优方法的最终验证困惑度，MMLU 0-shot任务提升7.2%，其他基准也取得一致提升。

Conclusion: HDS通过多维度奖励函数和强化学习框架，同时提升了LLM预训练的训练效率和最终模型能力。

Abstract: The composition of training data, governed by the diversity of sources and their mixing strategy, is a cornerstone of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training. Online Data Mixing (ODM), the technique of adaptively adjusting data mixtures during training, has emerged as a promising direction to improve efficiency. However, existing methods are constrained by their reliance on a singular optimization perspective, which fundamentally overlooks the need for complex LLM pre-training to consider the dynamic data composition from multiple dimensions. To overcome this limitation, we introduce the Holistic Data Scheduler (HDS), a novel online data mixing framework. HDS formulates the data scheduling challenge as a reinforcement learning problem in a continuous control space and leverages the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm for its stability and sample efficiency in exploring the high-dimensional policy space. At the core of HDS lies a novel multi-objective, holistic reward function that integrates three critical perspectives: a data-driven reward for quality, a loss-driven reward capturing inter-domain influence, and a model-driven reward based on weight norms. To validate our design and determine its optimal configuration, we conducted systematic experiments on LLMs of various sizes. On The Pile benchmark, HDS reaches the final validation perplexity of the next best method with 44% fewer training iterations. Furthermore, it achieves a 7.2% improvement on the MMLU 0-shot task along with consistent gains on other benchmarks, showcasing its ability to enhance both training efficiency and final model capability.

</details>


### [130] [A Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation Sampler for Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24140)
*Feiyang Fu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间重参数化累积强度外推（TR-CIE）采样器，用于改进离散流匹配模型在有限函数评估次数下的采样质量，通过时间重参数化消除噪声调度引起的刚性，并利用历史缓存输出近似累积强度，无需额外模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配（DFM）在离散状态空间上提供生成建模框架，但实际采样常采用τ-跳跃等离散化方法，在有限函数评估次数（NFE）下高效采样方法研究不足。

Method: TR-CIE包含两个组件：(1) 基于调度的时间重参数化，根据噪声调度重新缩放时间网格，吸收调度相关的增长项并缓解终端采样阶段的刚性；(2) 累积强度外推更新规则，利用上一步缓存的模型输出作为历史项，改进非均匀时间网格上逐步累积强度的近似。

Result: 在合成任务、文本生成和文本到图像基准上的实验表明，TR-CIE在有限NFE下提升了采样质量，且每步仅需一次函数评估，与标准τ-跳跃采样器相比不增加额外模型评估。

Conclusion: TR-CIE为DFM在受限函数评估下的高效采样提供了解决方案，理论分析了累积强度的局部近似误差并建立了收敛性结果。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) provides a principled framework for generative modeling on discrete state spaces via continuous-time Markov chain dynamics. In practice, sampling for DFM commonly employs discretizations such as $τ$-leaping, yet efficient sampling methods under a limited number of function evaluations (NFE) remain less studied. To address this gap, we propose the Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation (TR-CIE) sampler, which aims to improve sampling quality when function evaluations are restricted. TR-CIE consists of two components. First, a schedule-based time reparameterization rescales the time grid according to the noise schedule. Under standard factorized DFM rate parameterizations, this transformation of variables absorbs the schedule-dependent growth term and mitigates stiffness near the terminal sampling stage. Second, we introduce a cumulative-intensity extrapolation updating rule. By reusing cached model outputs from the previous step as a history term, this improves the approximation of stepwise cumulative intensities on the resulting non-uniform time grid. We provide a theoretical analysis that bounds the local approximation error of cumulative intensities and establishes convergence results. The resulting sampler requires one NFE per step and introduces no additional model evaluations compared to the standard $τ$-leaping sampler. Extensive experiments on synthetic tasks, text generation, and text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves sampling quality under limited NFE.

</details>


### [131] [AsyncOPD: How Stale Can On-Policy Distillation Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24143)
*Wonjun Kang,Kevin Galim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjun Kang,Sanghyun Park,Donghoon Kim,Minjae Lee,Minseo Kim,Rishabh Tiwari,Yuchen Zeng,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究异步在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的陈旧策略问题，发现KL方向影响陈旧数据鲁棒性（前向KL鲁棒，反向KL脆弱），并提出AsyncOPD异步训练框架，通过重算反向KL信号和多样本蒙特卡洛估计等方法，在保持精度的同时将训练吞吐量提升1.6-3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏面临on-policy系统瓶颈，rollout生成占主导训练时间。异步训练可缓解瓶颈但引入陈旧策略数据。虽然异步RL中的陈旧数据已被研究，但OPD中的影响尚未被探索。

Method: 1) 系统研究KL方向对陈旧数据鲁棒性的影响：前向KL鲁棒，反向KL脆弱；2) 验证异步RL稳定化方法在OPD中无效，提出更简单的OPD替代方案——在学习器端重算反向KL信号；3) 分析有限教师分数缓存对稀疏/采样反向KL估计器的偏差-方差权衡，提出多样本蒙特卡洛估计；4) 构建AsyncOPD全异步训练管线。

Result: AsyncOPD在保持与严格同步训练相当精度的前提下，将训练吞吐量提升1.6倍到3.8倍。

Conclusion: 异步OPD可通过合适的估计器设计和信号重算策略有效缓解on-policy瓶颈，前向KL更适合异步场景，而反向KL需要额外处理才能稳定。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts guided by teacher feedback and is becoming increasingly important for large language model (LLM) post-training. Like reinforcement learning (RL), however, OPD faces an on-policy systems bottleneck, as rollouts can dominate training time for reasoning workloads. Asynchronous training pipelines can alleviate this bottleneck by decoupling rollout generation from learner updates, but doing so introduces stale-policy data. While prior work has studied stale data in asynchronous RL, its effects in OPD remain underexplored. We present the first systematic study of staleness in asynchronous OPD, focusing on a practical setting where teacher feedback is implemented through local KL losses and full-vocabulary teacher logits are too expensive to store or transfer, necessitating finite teacher-score caches. We first show that KL direction changes the stale-data problem: teacher-weighted forward KL is more robust to stale rollouts, whereas student-weighted reverse KL is vulnerable. Second, for this vulnerable reverse-KL case, we study whether methods designed to stabilize asynchronous RL can mitigate OPD staleness. In our experiments, they do not improve over a simpler OPD-specific surrogate: recomputing the reverse-KL signal under the current student at learner time. Third, we analyze how finite teacher-score caches create a bias-variance tradeoff for sparse and sampled reverse-KL OPD estimators. This motivates multi-sample Monte Carlo (MC), which preserves MC correctability while reducing one-sample variance. Finally, we present and open-source AsyncOPD, a fully asynchronous OPD training pipeline built from these estimator choices. Experiments show that AsyncOPD improves training throughput by $1.6\times$ to $3.8\times$ over strict synchronous training while reaching comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [132] [Lightweight Transformer Models for On-Device Fault Detection: A Benchmark Study on Resource-Constrained Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24173)
*Disha Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对资源受限硬件上的故障检测任务，系统比较了传统ML方法（随机森林、XGBoost、SVM、逻辑回归）与轻量级Transformer（DistilBERT、TinyBERT-6L、TinyBERT-4L、MobileBERT）在三个公开数据集上的性能、模型大小和延迟。结果显示，在良好分离的数据上Transformer可匹敌传统ML但代价巨大；INT8量化可减少25%体积；自适应推理管线可在低延迟下保持高F1；而在严重不平衡数据集上所有方法均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署机器学习模型进行实时故障检测时，需要在精度、延迟和模型大小之间进行权衡，但目前缺乏对传统ML方法与轻量级Transformer在该场景下的系统比较。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS、SECOM和UCI AI4I 2020三个数据集上，对Random Forest、XGBoost、SVM、Logistic Regression与DistilBERT、TinyBERT-6L、TinyBERT-4L、MobileBERT进行二分类故障检测基准测试，评估F1分数、AUC、模型大小和CPU推理延迟，并进一步测试INT8动态量化和两阶段自适应推理管线。

Result: 在C-MAPSS上轻量级Transformer（87.8% F1）可匹配传统ML，但模型大小大100倍、延迟高9000倍；TinyBERT-4L是最部署友好的Transformer（55MB, 18ms）；INT8量化减少25%尺寸且保持86.9% F1；自适应管线路由97.9%预测通过量化轻量模型，仅2.1%交由大模型，达到87.6% F1和19.5ms平均延迟；在SECOM和UCI-PM上所有方法均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 轻量级Transformer在数据质量良好时可匹配传统ML但资源代价过高；INT8量化和自适应推理是有效的优化策略；当前方法在极端类别不平衡的故障检测场景中存在根本性局限。

Abstract: On-device fault detection enables real-time diagnostics without cloud dependency, but deploying machine learning models on resource-constrained hardware demands careful tradeoffs between accuracy, latency, and model size. We present a benchmark comparing traditional ML methods (Random Forest, XGBoost, SVM, Logistic Regression) against lightweight transformer architectures (DistilBERT, TinyBERT-6L, TinyBERT-4L, MobileBERT) for binary fault detection across three public datasets: NASA C-MAPSS turbofan degradation, SECOM semiconductor manufacturing, and UCI AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance. We evaluate classification performance (F1-score, AUC), model size, and CPU inference latency, and further assess INT8 dynamic quantization and a two-stage adaptive inference pipeline. Our results reveal that on well-separated sensor data (C-MAPSS), lightweight transformers match traditional ML at 87.8% F1 but at 100x the model size and 9000x the latency. TinyBERT-4L emerges as the most deployment-friendly transformer at 55 MB and 18 ms CPU latency. INT8 quantization reduces size by 25% while preserving 86.9% F1. Our adaptive pipeline, routing 97.9% of predictions through a quantized triage model and only 2.1% to a larger expert, achieves 87.6% F1 at 19.5 ms average latency. On severely imbalanced datasets (SECOM, UCI-PM), both traditional and transformer methods struggle significantly, highlighting fundamental limitations of current approaches for extreme class imbalance in fault detection. All code is publicly available.

</details>


### [133] [Project Ariadne: Prompt-Conditioned Route Generation for Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24184)
*Anton Morgunov,Victor S. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ariadne，一个解码器-only的路线生成器，将逆合成规划建模为提示-补全序列生成，支持通过提示字段灵活施加约束（如深度和所需起始原料），在多个基准上显著优于或媲美现有方法，并大幅降低了推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆合成规划方法要么依赖迭代搜索（计算成本高），要么直接生成路线但需要为不同规划约束训练独立模型，缺乏一个统一的、可灵活适应多种约束的端到端生成框架。

Method: Ariadne采用仅解码器架构，将目标分子、可选约束和完整合成路线统一编码为提示-完成序列；通过简单的提示字段设计（如路线深度、所需起始原料）实现约束条件的灵活注入，无需修改模型架构或训练独立模型。

Result: 在RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160基准上，24层单检查点通过深度约束提示将Solv-0提升13.7点，通过所需叶子约束提示提升31.2点；在所需叶子约束下的Top-10和Solv-0优于DESP，推理时间仅24 GPU分钟 vs 6.8 GPU小时；与DMS Explorer XL性能相当但推理时间减半；在目标-only基准上，在路线留出重建任务上增益最明显，但AiZynthFinder MCTS在部分Solv-0对比上仍更强。

Conclusion: Ariadne将序列生成从专用逆合成模型扩展到了提示条件化的结构化路线生成，证明了通过提示工程实现灵活约束的可行性；但当前缺少Tier-1--3路线检查器仍是该类模型对实验化学家有用的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning seeks to connect a target molecule to commercially available starting materials through a multistep route. Classical planners construct such routes by iteratively applying single-step reaction models within a search procedure; constrained variants often require specialized algorithms or architectural changes. Direct route generation reframes retrosynthesis as sequence generation, but existing direct-generation methods still train separate models for different planning specifications. We introduce Ariadne, a decoder-only route generator that represents the target, optional constraints, and route in one prompt-completion sequence. On the RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160 benchmark family, one 24-layer checkpoint follows route-depth and required-starting-material prompts: adding the corresponding prompt fields raises Solv-0 by 13.7 points for depth constraints and 31.2 points for required-leaf constraints. Ariadne also improves over DESP, a bidirectional search planner, on required-leaf Top-10 and Solv-0 in 24 GPU-minutes versus 6.8 GPU-hours. On standard reconstruction, Ariadne is comparable to DMS Explorer XL at about half the reported inference time. Across additional target-only benchmarks, Ariadne's clearest gains are on route-holdout reconstruction, whereas AiZynthFinder MCTS remains stronger on several Solv-0 comparisons. These results extend sequence generation from specialist retrosynthesis models to prompt-conditioned structural route generation. We release the codebase and training scripts to support further work, but do not introduce Tier-1--3 route checkers; those remain the main bottleneck before models of this kind can become useful to experimental chemists.

</details>


### [134] [Managing Task Execution for Unknown Workloads in Batteryless IoT: A Hardware-Agnostic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24340)
*Samer Nasser,Henrique Duarte Moura,Ritesh Kumar Singh,Maarten Weyn,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电池less物联网设备提出两种硬件无关的动态调度策略（无模型强化学习代理和近似预测方法），在物理精确仿真框架下与自适应任务速率方法及优化静态阈值进行对比，揭示了各方法在不同能量缓冲容量下的操作权衡：AP方法提供轻量级近最优吞吐量，RL代理实现可调生存-执行平衡，AsTAR擅长长能量间隙的执行节奏，而大电容设备可采用简单静态策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能量感知调度器依赖静态执行阈值或预测量的硬件特定任务配置文件，难以应对不可预测的工作负载，亟需硬件无关的通用动态调度策略来支持复杂边缘应用在能量采集架构下的可靠运行。

Method: 提出两种硬件无关的黑盒动态调度策略：无模型强化学习代理和即时近似预测方法；基于真实太阳能数据和动态LoRa传输配置构建物理精确仿真框架；与自适应任务速率方法及静态优化阈值进行对比评估。

Result: AP方法实现轻量级近最优任务吞吐量；RL代理提供可调节的生存-执行权衡；AsTAR在长能量间隙下执行节奏表现优异；大缓冲容量设备可采用简单静态策略。

Conclusion: 不同调度策略具有明确的操作权衡，无通用最优方案；高级策略对严重受限的小电容系统至关重要，而大能量缓冲设备可依靠更简单的静态策略。

Abstract: In recent years, the Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm has been shifting toward batteryless, energy-harvesting architectures. Sustaining reliable operation in these systems requires intelligent management of highly volatile stored energy. As edge applications grow in complexity, traditional energy-aware schedulers struggle with unpredictable workloads due to their reliance on static execution thresholds or pre-measured, hardware-specific task profiles. To overcome this, we propose two novel, hardware-agnostic dynamic scheduling strategies treating applications as a "black box," requiring no prior energy information: a model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent and an on-the-fly Approximated Prediction (AP) method. We evaluate these methods against an adaptive task rate approach (AsTAR) and optimized static thresholds using a custom-built, physically accurate simulation framework driven by real-world solar data and dynamic LoRa transmission profiles. Rather than claiming universal superiority, our analysis exposes the distinct operational trade-offs of each method: the AP approach delivers lightweight, near-oracle task throughput; the RL agent provides tunable survival-execution balancing; and AsTAR excels at execution pacing across long energy gaps. Finally, we demonstrate that while these advanced strategies provide critical resilience for severely constrained systems with small capacitors, devices with larger energy buffers can efficiently rely on simpler, less computationally expensive static policies.

</details>


### [135] [Parallel Manifold Steering: Efficient Adaptation of Large Associative Memories via Residual Energy Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24396)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H-Res（层级残差引导），通过在激活流形上学习状态依赖向量场来调控Transformer的能量景观，在不修改全局权重或增加序列长度的情况下实现任务适配，比LoRA和VPT更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在塑性-稳定性困境：直接修改权重（如LoRA）会导致灾难性干扰，而静态提示（如VPT）会降低关联记忆容量。需要一种既不破坏预训练记忆系统、又不增加计算开销的适配方法。

Method: 将Transformer视为致密关联记忆（DAM），将适配问题建模为激活流形上的控制问题，学习状态依赖向量场来引导token轨迹进入任务特定吸引子盆地，称为层级残差引导（H-Res）。

Result: 在关联检索任务上比全局权重修改方法提升26%性能，消除了基于提示方法的计算开销，能有效扩展到结构化领域。

Conclusion: H-Res通过调控能量景观而非修改权重或扩展序列长度，在保持基础模型注意力熵的同时促进神经坍缩，为冻住的大模型提供了一种高效且无损的适配方案。

Abstract: Large Transformer models function as Dense Associative Memories (DAMs), retrieving knowledge via high-dimensional attractor dynamics driven by the self-attention mechanism \citep{ramsauer2020hopfield, wu2024attention}. However, adapting these frozen memory systems to new tasks presents a fundamental ``Plasticity-Stability'' dilemma. Current methods either risk catastrophic interference by modifying synaptic weights directly (e.g., LoRA) \citep{hu2021lora} or degrade associative capacity by clogging the retrieval buffer with static prompt tokens (e.g., VPT) \citep{jia2022vpt}. In this work, we propose \textbf{H-Res} (Hierarchical Residual Steering), a mechanism that modulates the effective energy landscape of the Transformer without altering its global equilibrium or expanding its sequence length. By formulating adaptation as a control problem on the activation manifold \citep{chen2018neuralode}, H-Res learns a state-dependent vector field that steers token trajectories into task-specific basins of attraction. We formally prove that H-Res preserves the attention entropy of the foundation model and facilitates Neural Collapse \citep{papyan2020prevalence}. Empirically, Manifold Steering outperforms global weight modification by 26\% on associative retrieval tasks and eliminates the computational overhead of prompt-based methods, scaling effectively to structured domains \citep{zha2023vtab}.

</details>


### [136] [Natural Identifiers for Privacy and Data Audits in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24408)
*Lorenzo Rossi,Bartłomiej Marek,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自然标识符（NIDs）——如加密哈希和短URL等结构化随机字符串，用于解决LLM隐私审计中依赖特制金丝雀数据或私有留出数据集的难题，支持无需重训练的后验差分隐私审计和数据集推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私审计方法要么需要训练时插入特制金丝雀数据（无法用于已训练模型），要么数据集推断需要同分布的私有非成员留出数据集（现实中难以获取），严重制约了可扩展的后验审计能力。

Method: 利用LLM训练数据中自然存在的结构化随机字符串（如加密哈希、缩短URL）作为NIDs，利用其格式可生成无限同分布随机字符串，分别作为替代金丝雀用于差分隐私审计，以及作为同分布留出数据用于数据集推断。

Result: 实验表明，使用NIDs可以在无需重训练的情况下实现后验差分隐私审计，并且对于任何包含NIDs的可疑数据集均可进行数据集推断，无需私有非成员留出数据集。

Conclusion: NIDs为LLM隐私审计提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，克服了现有方法对特制金丝雀数据和私有留出数据的依赖，实现了真正的后验审计能力。

Abstract: Assessing the privacy of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges. In particular, most existing methods for auditing differential privacy require the insertion of specially crafted canary data during training, making them impractical for auditing already-trained models without costly retraining. Additionally, dataset inference, which audits whether a suspect dataset was used to train a model, is infeasible without access to a private non-member held-out dataset. Yet, such held-out datasets are often unavailable or difficult to construct for real-world cases since they have to be from the same distribution (IID) as the suspect data. These limitations severely hinder the ability to conduct scalable, post-hoc audits. To enable such audits, this work introduces natural identifiers (NIDs) as a novel solution to the above-mentioned challenges. NIDs are structured random strings, such as cryptographic hashes and shortened URLs, naturally occurring in common LLM training datasets. Their format enables the generation of unlimited additional random strings from the same distribution, which can act as alternative canaries for audits and as same-distribution held-out data for dataset inference. Our evaluation highlights that indeed, using NIDs, we can facilitate post-hoc differential privacy auditing without any retraining and enable dataset inference for any suspect dataset containing NIDs without the need for a private non-member held-out dataset.

</details>


### [137] [An LLM-based Two-Stage Transformer Framework for Cross-Domain Bearing Fault Diagnosis with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24459)
*Jinghan Wang,Feng Cheng,Wentao Wu,Hang Li,Gaoliang Peng,Tianchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架，使用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer结合因果自注意力从振动信号中提取层次特征，通过多源预训练和原型知识调制实现跨工况、跨数据集的轴承故障诊断，在仅用10%标注目标数据的情况下达到92.61%的平均准确率，比现有最先进方法高出17.24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轴承故障诊断方法面临数据集异构、工况变化与标注数据稀缺并存的实际工业挑战，但现有方法将这些挑战孤立处理且依赖隐式特征对齐，在并发挑战下效果受限。

Method: 提出知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架：(1) 第一阶段使用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer（因果自注意力）进行多源预训练，学习可泛化的层次特征表示；(2) 第二阶段通过原型知识调制（prototype-based knowledge modulation）进行目标域适配，并采用分类学自适应分类（taxonomy-adaptive classification）实现跨异构故障类别的无缝迁移。预训练编码器权重和故障原型嵌入作为知识载体。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，仅使用10%标注目标数据即达到92.61%的平均准确率，比现有最先进方法性能提升17.24个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业4.0场景下具有成本效益的预测性维护提供了实用路径，有效解决了数据异构、工况变化与标签稀缺的联合挑战。

Abstract: Bearing fault diagnosis faces critical challenges when dataset heterogeneity, operating condition variations, and limited labeled data occur simultaneously in industrial environments. Existing approaches address these issues in isolation and rely on implicit feature alignment, limiting effectiveness under concurrent challenges. This paper proposes a knowledge-guided two-stage transfer learning framework that employs a lightweight GPT-2-style Transformer with causal self-attention for hierarchical feature extraction from vibration signals, establishing explicit pathways where pre-trained encoder weights and fault prototype embeddings serve as knowledge carriers from multi-source pre-training to target adaptation. The framework addresses the dual-shift challenge through multi-source learning for generalizable representations, prototype-based knowledge modulation for target adaptation, and taxonomy-adaptive classification for seamless transfer across heterogeneous fault categories. Experimental validation on four real-world datasets demonstrates 92.61% average accuracy with only 10% labeled target data, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 17.24 percentage points, establishing a practical pathway toward cost-effective predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 applications.

</details>


### [138] [A Fair Evaluation of Graph Foundation Models for Node Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24509)
*Oleg Platonov,Gleb Bazhenov,Dmitry Eremeev,Liudmila Prokhorenkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对9种面向节点属性预测的图基础模型（GFM）进行了公平且严格的重新评估，发现只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新型GFM在预测性能上超越调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFM）在节点属性预测任务上受到广泛关注，但该领域缺乏统一的评估标准，不同工作采用差异化的评估方式，导致GFM之间以及与其他模型之间无法进行可靠比较。

Method: 对9种近期提出的GFM进行公平且严格的重新评估，与强GNN基线进行比较。

Result: 只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新型GFM在预测性能上优于调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。

Conclusion: 现有GFM中仅少数能在性能上超越强GNN基线，且存在推理效率的权衡，领域评估标准亟需统一。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [139] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从集体计算视角将LLM重新定义为高维稠密联想记忆模型，提出基于吉布斯测度的谱熵检索机制，通过多路径采样与能量加权使模型"松弛"到稳定吸引子盆地，在GSM8K上将Phi-3.5提升5.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将LLM视为自回归生成器，忽略了其作为联想记忆模型的内在几何特性。作者希望利用数学推理的能量景观——正确推理链对应宽深吸引子盆地（平坦最小值），而幻觉对应尖锐不稳定局部最小值——来改进推理质量。

Method: 提出基于吉布斯测度的谱熵检索机制：采样多条推理路径，根据路径的逆能量加权（P ∝ e^{-βE}），通过"松弛"过程使系统逼近联想记忆的平衡分布，从而进入鲁棒的吸引子盆地。

Result: 在GSM8K数据集上，Microsoft Phi-3.5模型性能从84.7%提升至90.1%，提升5.38个百分点。

Conclusion: 推理过程更适合建模为动态沉降到吸引子盆地的过程，而非贪婪的下一词预测。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [140] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QC-SMOTE提出了一种质量可控的过采样框架，通过复合邻域可信度评分评估少数类样本可靠性，结合IPQ引导的最佳K策略生成合成样本，并依据局部数据几何特征自适应调整生成行为，在30个不平衡数据集上取得了最优的AUC-ROC和Macro F1表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过采样方法（如SMOTE）在噪声或类重叠区域容易生成低质量的合成样本，影响分类性能。

Method: 提出QC-SMOTE框架，包含：(1) 复合邻域可信度评分（融合局部密度、安全级别和与多数类的隔离度）；(2) IPQ引导的最佳K策略（评估中点纯度及多数类清除）；(3) 基于样本可靠性和边界信息性的分配机制；(4) 跨重叠-不平衡机制的自适应生成行为；(5) 低质量样本回退为原始少数类副本的优雅降级机制。

Result: 在30个不平衡数据集上使用重复分层交叉验证，QC-SMOTE在AUC-ROC和Macro F1指标上均达到最强平均表现，在中度和严重不平衡场景下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 质量感知和几何自适应合成采样对于鲁棒的不平衡分类至关重要，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [141] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowPipe提出了一种基于条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）的统一框架，将机器学习数据准备管线构建转化为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成问题，通过轨迹平衡目标、深度语义调制（融入LLM先验）和失败感知机制，解决了现有Multi-DQN方法在长程信用分配、数据集上下文注入和无效状态探索三方面的局限性，在74个真实数据集上平均准确率提升11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA方法（Multi-DQN）存在三个关键局限：解耦的价值估计器削弱了长程信用分配、数据集上下文仅被弱注入策略、在存在大量无效状态的稀疏搜索空间中探索效率低下。因此需要一种能够统一解决这些问题的框架。

Method: FlowPipe将管线构建建模为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成，使用条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）配合轨迹平衡（Trajectory Balance）目标，将终端验证奖励与早期管线决策联系起来；通过特征向线性调制（FiLM）引入深度语义调制，使LLM推导的逻辑先验能够根据数据集语义条件化策略的内部激活；并在流目标中融入失败感知机制以避免无效状态。

Result: 在两个基准套件、74个真实数据集上的实验表明，FlowPipe优于SOTA基线方法，平均准确率提升11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。

Conclusion: FlowPipe通过统一的条件概率流生成框架有效解决了数据准备管线构建中的关键挑战，显著提升了性能与训练效率。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [142] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Grad Detect 通过分析推理过程中单次前向-反向传播的逐层梯度模式来预测 LLM 幻觉，在 QA 基准上优于置信度基线和采样基线，并发现最后五层集中了 97% 以上的判别性梯度信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在生成时容易产生幻觉，在高风险应用中对幻觉的可靠检测至关重要，而现有输出级信号方法无法充分捕捉模型内部关于输出正确性的信息。

Method: 提出 Grad Detect，通过在单次前向-反向传播中分析层间梯度模式来预测幻觉；跨四个架构家族共十一个模型进行层消融实验。

Result: 在幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务上持续优于置信度基线和采样基线；最后五层集中了超过 97% 的判别性梯度信号。

Conclusion: Grad Detect 为预测 LLM 可靠性的多个维度提供了统一框架，兼具强预测性能和可解释的模型失败源头洞察。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [143] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hartley 神经算子（HNO），作为傅里叶神经算子（FNO）的纯实数镜像，用离散 Hartley 变换替代 FFT。核心发现：椭圆型算子（Poisson、双调和）的实对称格林函数更适合实数 Hartley 基，而时间依赖算子（波动、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）的相位信息更适合复数 Fourier 基，热方程是分界线。不存在普适最优，应依据算子对称性匹配谱基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO 对实值 PDE 解使用复数 FFT 存在共轭对称带来的表示冗余，作者希望引入纯实数变换消除冗余，并探究不同 PDE 算子应匹配何种谱基。

Method: 将 FNO 中的 FFT 替换为纯实数的离散 Hartley 变换（DHT），每个保留谱模式学习一个实数权重，无需复数运算。HNO 与 FNO 在同宽度下等参数，仅谱基不同。

Result: 椭圆型算子（Poisson、双调和）HNO 更优；时间依赖算子（波动、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）FNO 更优；热方程是分界线。这种分界线与算子相位含量单调相关，符合格林函数理论。

Conclusion: 不存在通用最优神经算子，应根据 PDE 解算子的对称性匹配谱基：实对称格林函数用实数 Hartley 基，含相位信息用复数 Fourier 基。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Automated Residual Plot Assessment With the R Package autovi and the Shiny Application autovi.web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24236)
*Weihao Li,Dianne Cook,Emi Tanaka,Susan VanderPlas,Klaus Ackermann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个R包，利用计算机视觉模型自动化评估线性模型的残差图，并提供了Shiny应用辅助分析师判断模型拟合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统残差图可视化评估依赖人工判断，存在可扩展性差、分析者间决策不一致的问题；而lineup协议虽能减少主观性，但需要更多人力投入。因此需要自动化方案。

Method: 开发了一个R包，内置计算机视觉模型用于自动化评估残差图；同时提供了Shiny应用以降低使用门槛。模型输入为残差样本，输出为视觉信号强度（VSS）及辅助解释信息。

Result: 模型能够预测视觉信号强度（VSS）并提供支持信息，帮助分析师评估模型拟合度。

Conclusion: 该自动化方法可替代人工残差图评估，在提升可扩展性的同时保持评估一致性，适用于数据驱动场景下的线性模型诊断。

Abstract: Visual assessment of residual plots is a common approach for diagnosing linear models, but it relies on manual evaluation, which does not scale well and can lead to inconsistent decisions across analysts. The lineup protocol, which embeds the observed plot among null plots, can reduce subjectivity but requires even more human effort. In today's data-driven world, such tasks are well suited for automation. We present a new R package that uses a computer vision model to automate the evaluation of residual plots. An accompanying Shiny application is provided for ease of use. Given a sample of residuals, the model predicts a visual signal strength (VSS) and offers supporting information to help analysts assess model fit.

</details>


### [145] [Model selection with proper scoring rules on data sets of time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24715)
*Giorgio Corani,Stefano Damato,Dario Azzimonti,Lorenzo Zambon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间序列模型选择中不同汇总统计量（平均得分、中位得分、平均排名）在短测试集下会产生冲突决策，发现冲突源于得分分布的偏态，且随着测试集增大，各标准趋于一致，但在短测试集中仅平均得分能正确识别真实模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示时间序列模型选择中常用汇总统计量（平均得分、中位得分、平均排名）产生冲突决策的根本原因，并探究测试集长度对这些标准一致性的影响。

Method: 通过理论分析得分分布的偏态性解释冲突根源，并在间歇性时间序列数据（含M5竞赛数据集）上进行实证分析，比较不同测试集长度下各模型选择标准的表现。

Result: 1）冲突决策源于得分分布的偏态性；2）测试集增大时各标准收敛到相同结论；3）短测试集下仅平均得分能正确识别真实模型；4）平均排名不受缩放因子影响。

Conclusion: 在时间序列模型选择中，测试集长度是关键因素；短测试集时应使用平均得分而非中位得分或平均排名；建议采用大测试集以确保各标准结论一致。

Abstract: We consider the problem of model selection between probabilistic models on data sets of time series. Chosen a proper scoring rule, we denote by the term \textit{score} the average value of the scoring rule on the test of an individual time series. For model selection, we need aggregating the values of the scores across multiple time series. Three summary statistics are commonly used for model selection: mean score, median score, and mean rank. Results in previous papers show that these statistics can yield conflicting decisions; we show how the conflicting conclusions are due to the skewness of the distribution of scores. We also show that as the test set of each time series of the data set increases, the different model selection criteria progressively converge to the same conclusion. However, for short tests sets, only the mean score identifies the true model as the best.
  We illustrate these phenomena with an analysis on intermittent time series, including the data set of the M5 competition, where we underline the importance of having a large test set. In such experiments, we further notice that model selection based on mean ranks remains unchanged using different scaling factors.

</details>
